THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226 AND
227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO DECLARE
THAT SECTION 20(2)(a-iv) AND (a-v) THE KARNATAKA CO-OPERATIVE SOCIETIES ACT IS ULTRA VIRES THE LEGISLATIVE
COMPETENCE AND ARTICLE 243ZI AND 243ZO OF THE
CONSTITUTION OF INDIA AND ETC.
THIS WRIT PETITION COMING ON FOR PRELIMINARY
HEARING THIS DAY, THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:
ORDER

Heard Shri. Jai Prakash Reddy M., learned advocate
for the petitioners, Smt. Niloufer Akbar, learned AGA for R1,
R4, R5 & R7 and Sri. G.V. Shashikumar, Advocate for R2 &
R3.
2. Shri. Jai Prakash Reddy M., learned advocate submits
that elections have already been conducted. Therefore, this
petition is rendered infructuous and accordingly prayed for

278
its dismissal. He has filed a memo of even date to the said
effect. Memo is placed on record.
3. In the circumstances, petition is dismissed as having
become infructuous.
 No costs.
 Sd/-
 JUDGE
